# Magic engine bug ledger

| `rav-dimir-doppelganger-manifest-contract-stale` | RAV merge-integration defect — the Dimir Doppelganger promotion raised the typed full-fidelity manifest to 281 IDs, but the compile-time positive-manifest oracle still enumerated 280 IDs and omitted Savra. | Fixed; red `e2ad28d4`, green `2157bcd0` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-fast-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-rav --test catalog_coverage --test coverage_report_contract --test full_fidelity_simple_spells_contract --quiet`. Compilation failed with `can't compare [&str; 281] with [&str; 280]` at `tests/full_fidelity_simple_spells_contract.rs`; the set manifest contains `RAV-SAVRA-QUEEN-OF-THE-GOLGARI` but the literal oracle did not. Green adds that ID to the exact positive-manifest oracle; the 15 catalog, one coverage-report, and two full-fidelity contract tests pass. |
| `resumed-stack-resolution-target-legality-snapshot-missing` | Engine stack/target-resolution defect — a spell that had already started resolving recomputed its complete target plan after a mandatory no-priority private decision. If an earlier instruction moved a repeated target, the resumed plan became all-illegal and the valid decision submission rolled back instead of completing the remaining suffix. | Fixed; red `eb9628fa`, green `2e1380ea` | Exact red command: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-core-audit-green-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test resumed_resolution_target_snapshot_red -- --nocapture` from `varieties/magic`. The test casts a three-instruction instant targeting the same creature twice: it exiles that creature, pauses at a policy-submitted library search, then should skip the later stale target instruction. Before green, the valid selected-card submission returned `IllegalAction("a resumed stack item cannot become countered after beginning resolution")`; the trace ended `CardMoved(target, Exile) → DecisionOpened(LibrarySearch)`. Green retains the effect-aligned CR 608.2b target-legality snapshot exactly while the matching nonzero stack cursor and no-priority continuation are live. The repaired trace appends `DecisionCompleted → LibrarySearchResolved → TargetInstructionSkipped(effect_index: 2) → SpellResolved → CardMoved(source, Graveyard)`. The new regression plus multi-instruction library search, quantity replacement, targeted discard, target-player mana-color, and damage-replacement continuations pass 7/7; scoped strict Clippy, fmt, and diff checks pass. |
| `optional-trigger-prompts-before-all-illegal-target-check` | Engine stack/target-resolution defect — an optional targeted triggered ability opened its payment choice before the resolver checked its target plan. If a response made every declared target illegal, the engine prompted to pay instead of countering the ability by the rules under CR 608.2b. | Fixed; red `fcec9cdb`, green `7d8fbb8c` | Exact red command: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-core-audit-green-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test optional_trigger_all_illegal_target_red -- --nocapture` from `varieties/magic`. The source's optional upkeep damage trigger chose one creature target; the opponent exiled it in response. Before green the trace ended with the target exile and response terminal receipts, then `optional=Some(OptionalTriggeredAbilityChoiceView { ... })`. Green preflights the immutable stack target plan before an optional trigger may open its payment decision. The repaired trace instead appends `AbilityCounteredByRules(source, optional-upkeep-damage)` and leaves `optional=None`. Focused optional, target-choice, and RAV Savra/Searing/Frenzied checks pass 21/21; scoped strict Clippy, fmt, and diff checks pass. |

| `targeted-reanimate-creature-land-entry-trigger-missing` | Engine targeted-reanimation/land-entry defect — `ReturnTargetCreatureCardToBattlefieldWithCounterIfManaColorSpent` captured the returned creature's self ETB but omitted its land-entry event. A legal creature–land graveyard target entered with no represented `LandEntersBattlefield` trigger. | Fixed; red `55580df6`, green `1cffab32` | Exact red command: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-core-audit-green-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test reanimate_creature_land_entry_red -- --nocapture` from `varieties/magic`. The legal black-mana trace reached `CardMoved(creature-land, Battlefield) → SpellResolved → CardMoved(reanimate, Graveyard)`, then failed because `reanimated-creature-land-entry` never stacked. Green extracts the shared physical-permanent entry capture used by policy and deterministic one-card searches and calls it from targeted reanimation. The repaired event log appends `TriggeredAbilityStacked(reanimated-creature-land-entry)` only after the source spell's terminal graveyard move. Focused entry/search/reanimation and RAV Vigor/Farseek/Chord checks pass 20/20; scoped strict Clippy, fmt, and diff checks pass. |

| `delayed-return-creature-land-entry-trigger-missing` | Engine delayed-return/land-entry defect — the linked delayed-exile return captured generic ETB observations but never invoked land-entry capture. A returned represented creature–land therefore re-entered the battlefield with no `LandEntersBattlefield` trigger provenance. | Fixed; red `aa74f149`, green `78a5e3a0` | Exact red command: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-core-audit-green-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test delayed_return_creature_land_entry_red -- --nocapture` from `varieties/magic`. It failed at the expected returned creature–land trigger: the trace included `CardMoved(... Battlefield) → ObjectIncarnationAdvanced → DelayedActionConsumed`, but no `TriggeredAbilityStacked(returned-creature-land-entry)`. Green generalizes the simultaneous-entry capture helper used by delayed linked returns, matching returns, and multi-library search. The repaired trace appends `TriggeredAbilityStacked(returned-creature-land-entry)` immediately after `DelayedActionConsumed`. Focused simultaneous-entry/Bloodbond/Cloudstone/Chord checks pass 16/16; scoped strict Clippy, fmt, and diff checks pass. |

| `simultaneous-return-creature-land-entry-triggers-missing` | Engine simultaneous-graveyard-return/land-entry defect — matching creature cards returned together through the one-event path received generic ETB observations but never reached the separate land-entry trigger pipeline. Represented creature–land entrants therefore lost their own and peer land-entry triggers. | Fixed; red `ab96a015`, green `7545e5e0` | Exact red command: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-core-audit-green-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test simultaneous_return_creature_land_entry_red -- --nocapture` from `varieties/magic`. It moved two same-named creature–land cards from graveyards to the battlefield after a captured creature-cast trigger, then failed `left: 0`, `right: 4`; the trace had both `CardMoved(... Battlefield)` receipts and the return `AbilityResolved`, with no `observe-land-entry` receipt. Green queues each returned land's event after the one generic post-entry snapshot. Its event log records the return terminal receipt, Player 0's two-event `TriggeredAbilityOrder` decision and stack receipts, then the corresponding Player 1 decision and receipts. Focused simultaneous-entry/Bloodbond/Loxodon checks pass 8/8; scoped strict Clippy, fmt, and diff checks pass. |

| `multi-library-search-simultaneous-entry-observer-missing` | Engine library-search/simultaneous-entry defect — a multi-card search moved and captured selected battlefield entrants serially. A later selected permanent could not observe an earlier selected permanent's entry, although the cards enter through one multi-permanent event under CR 603.6a. | Fixed; red `f3ef27cb`, green `b1e9bc79` | Exact red command: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-core-audit-green-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test multi_library_search_simultaneous_entry_observer_red -- --nocapture` from `varieties/magic`. It failed `left: 1`, `right: 2`: the deterministic selection moved Body, then Watcher, but only Watcher's self-observation was queued. Green commits every deterministic or policy-selected battlefield result before applying each entrant's pre-event replacement sources and then takes one post-event trigger snapshot. The repaired deterministic and policy traces both emit two `TriggeredAbilityOrder` candidates for Watcher and, after the submitted order, two `TriggeredAbilityStacked` receipts. Focused core search tests pass 7/7; dependent Chord/Farseek/Civic RAV search tests pass 10/10; scoped strict Clippy, fmt, and diff checks pass. |

| `linked-delayed-return-simultaneous-entry-observer-missing` | Engine delayed-action/simultaneous-entry defect — a linked creature-and-Aura return moved and captured members serially. The Aura therefore never observed its companion creature entering, although CR 603.6a checks all battlefield permanents, including newcomers, after an event puts multiple permanents onto the battlefield. | Fixed; red `7078b7b9`, green `8fc0e1cb` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-simultaneous-linked-return-entry-red-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test simultaneous_linked_return_entry_observer_red -- --nocapture`. It failed `left: 1`, `right: 2`: after both return `CardMoved(... Battlefield)` receipts and `DelayedActionConsumed`, only the Aura's own observer was stacked. Green captures every entrant's self ETB plus controlled nonartifact/Aura observers from one post-event snapshot. The repaired trace opens `TriggeredAbilityOrder` with two Aura occurrences; its submitted order emits `TriggeredAbilityOrderChosen` and `TriggeredAbilityStacked` twice. The same shared substrate now governs simultaneous matching-creature graveyard returns. Focused linked/delayed/Aura tests pass 4/4; Bloodbond March and Cloudstone dependent contracts pass 7/7; scoped strict Clippy, fmt, and diff checks pass. |

| `retained-copied-ability-stack-definition-lost` | Engine stack/provenance defect — an activated ability explicitly retained by a copy effect may be activated from the copied form, but the stack invariant re-derived its definition from the copied characteristics. Dimir Doppelganger copying Watchwolf therefore placed its retained physical ability on the stack and then rolled the legal activation back because Watchwolf has no such binding. | Fixed; red `4fa09004`, green `7a6eca2a` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-doppelganger-green-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-rav --test dimir_doppelganger_red -- --nocapture`. Before green, `doppelganger_copies_any_graveyard_creature_then_retains_its_physical_ability` failed with `IllegalAction("stack ability is not bound to its source definition")`; the first copy/exile succeeded and only the retained repeat activation rolled back. Green adds immutable `StackObject::ability_definition` provenance emitted with every nonmana activation and requires its matching `AbilityActivated` receipt before validating the frozen binding. The repeated-copy trace now reaches the second `AbilityManaPaid → AbilityActivated(definition=RAV-DIMIR-DOPPELGANGER) → CardMoved(target, Exile) → PermanentCopied → AbilityResolved`; the copied 0/0 regression also proves `PermanentCopied < AbilityResolved < StateBasedAction`. |

| `aura-search-entry-observer-missing` | Engine source-bound Aura-search/entry-observer defect — the compatible-Aura library-search continuation queued only the fetched Aura's self-ETB, omitting other controlled permanents that observe an Aura entering. | Fixed; red `5280c87d`, green `297e821d` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-aura-search-entry-observer-red-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test aura_search_entry_observer_red -- --nocapture`. A creature's ETB searches for and attaches a compatible Aura while its controller has a legal optional `ControlledAuraEntersBattlefield` token trigger. Before green the trace ended `CardMoved(Aura, Battlefield) → AttachmentEstablishedWithoutContinuousEffect → LibrarySearchResolved → LibraryShuffled → AbilityResolved`; the observer never stacked. The repair uses the common post-attachment entry capture, preserving both the Aura's self trigger and all controlled Aura/nonartifact observer events until the original search ability records its terminal receipt. Green appends `TriggeredAbilityStacked(source watcher, controlled-aura-entered)` after `AbilityResolved`; generic Aura/search and RAV Auratouched Mage/Bramble Elemental contracts pass. |

| `deterministic-multi-search-entry-etb-missing` | Engine deterministic multi-search/entry-trigger defect — the compatibility `SearchControllerLibraryMany` resolver moved selected permanents to the battlefield but skipped their entry-capture pipeline. A short-lived selected creature reached the enclosing spell's mandatory SBA death with no historical ETB event. | Fixed; red `da1b5183`, green `462d531b` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-deterministic-multi-search-entry-red-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test deterministic_multi_search_entry_etb_red -- --nocapture`. A zero-cost instant uses deterministic multi-search to find one 0/0 creature with a target-free ETB. Before green the trace was `CardMoved(creature, Battlefield) → LibrarySearchBatchResolved → LibraryShuffled → SpellResolved → StateBasedAction(zero toughness) → CardMoved(creature, Graveyard)` with no `TriggeredAbilityStacked`. The deterministic Battlefield and BattlefieldTapped branches now use the shared selected-permanent entry capture before the enclosing spell terminal/SBA boundary. Green retains `source_incarnation: 2` across the selected creature's SBA death and then records `TriggeredAbilityStacked(..., fragile-etb)`; deterministic/policy search, Chord of Calling, and Farseek contracts pass. |

| `delayed-return-entry-etb-missing` | Engine delayed-action/entry-trigger defect — an end-step linked-exile return moved its primary creature from exile to the battlefield but never captured or flushed that entry's own and observer ETB triggers. | Fixed; red `33400eb7`, green `ec3e3b4b` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-delayed-return-entry-red-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test delayed_return_entry_etb_red -- --nocapture`. A legal blink exiles a creature with a target-free ETB, then the scheduled end-step action returns it. Before green the end-step suffix was `StepBegan(End) → CardMoved(creature, Battlefield) → ObjectIncarnationAdvanced → DelayedActionConsumed` with no `TriggeredAbilityStacked`. The repair captures every returned creature or Aura after its final entry/attachment state, reaches one shared delayed-action SBA boundary, then flushes the batch. Green appends `TriggeredAbilityStacked(controller Player 1, source creature, incarnation 3, returned-creature-etb)` after `DelayedActionConsumed`; related delayed-return and Voyager Staff contracts pass. |

| `no-cast-aura-entry-etb-missing` | Engine attachment/entry-trigger defect — public `enter_attachment_without_cast` moved a legal Aura to the battlefield and established its attachment, but never captured the Aura's own ETB or controller-scoped entry observers before its post-entry SBA boundary. | Fixed; red `3ca155ff`, green `fa2b075a` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-no-cast-aura-entry-red-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test no_cast_aura_entry_etb_red -- --nocapture`. A legal no-cast Aura entry has a target-free life-gain ETB. Before green the complete trace was `CardMoved(Aura, Battlefield) → ObjectIncarnationAdvanced → AttachmentEstablishedWithoutContinuousEffect`; the public transition returned success with no `TriggeredAbilityStacked`. The repair captures self and controller-scoped entry observations after the Aura is legally attached but before its SBA checkpoint, then flushes them only afterward. Green records `TriggeredAbilityStacked(source Aura, incarnation 2, aura-entry-life)` after the attachment receipt; pregame fixture construction deliberately remains non-triggering. Attachment, begin-game, Dream Leash, Galvanic Arc, and Mark of Eviction contracts pass. |
| `savra-sacrifice-color-trigger-missing` | RAV/core coverage gap — collector `#225` was catalog-only because the engine had no controller-sacrifices-a-creature-of-color trigger, optional life payment at trigger resolution, or serial each-opponent creature-sacrifice decision. | Fixed; red `ac680d35`, green `aa4b16ed` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-rav-savra-red-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-rav --test savra_red -- --nocapture`; before green it failed at the executable boundary with `CapabilityGap { collector_number: 225, name: "Savra, Queen of the Golgari", capability_gap: "card-specific-rules-not-implemented" }`. Green adds color-qualified sacrifice observation before the sacrificed creature changes zones, explicit APNAP ordering for a black-and-green sacrifice, an optional two-life payment before every remaining opponent publicly selects a creature to sacrifice, and an independent optional green two-life gain with no `LifePaid` receipt. The focused 5/5 trace records `TriggeredAbilityOrderChosen`, both Savra stack receipts, black pay/decline branches, green gain/decline branches, and the selected opponent sacrifice; every branch ends with `validate_invariants()` passing. |

| `dimir-doppelganger-graveyard-copy-missing` | RAV/core coverage gap — collector `#202` was catalog-only because the engine could copy a live permanent or an entering permanent, but could not exile one targeted creature card from any public graveyard, snapshot its copiable values, apply them to the activated source, and retain the source's own activated ability in its copied form. | Fixed; red `6567203a`, substrate `10fd57a2`, core red `48873755`, green `efffc957` | Exact card red command: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-doppelganger-red-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-rav --test dimir_doppelganger_red -- --nocapture`; it failed at the executable boundary with `CapabilityGap(...card-specific-rules-not-implemented)`. After the public copy substrate, the separate core red rerun failed `doppelganger_copies_any_graveyard_creature_then_retains_its_physical_ability` with `IllegalAction("stack ability is not bound to its source definition")`. Green adds a public-graveyard creature target, layer-one copiable snapshot, exile-and-copy effect, retained physical activated ability, immutable stack binding provenance, and terminal ability/SBA ordering. Focused Dimir tests pass 3/3; the full-fidelity partition is 281 full / 6 partial / 4 catalog-only unique names. |

| `sisters-of-stone-death-source-linked-combat-missing` | RAV/core coverage gap — collector `#231` was catalog-only because the engine could not bind a target creature to block one exact source this turn if able, prove an exile target is blocking or blocked by that same source, and retain source-linked exiled creature-card provenance for later battlefield entry under the source controller. | Fixed; red `f0f7216b`, green `f3d8e024` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-rav-sisters-red-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-rav --test sisters_of_stone_death_red -- --nocapture`; before green it returned `Err(CapabilityGap { collector_number: 231, name: "Sisters of Stone Death", capability_gap: "card-specific-rules-not-implemented" })` rather than `Ok("RAV-SISTERS-OF-STONE-DEATH")`. Green adds current-combat source-relative targeting, an end-of-turn exact-pair block requirement, exact exile-incarnation source linkage, and a public one-of-current-candidates return decision. Zone departure prunes the matching card or source entitlement, and an empty eligible set resolves the target-free return as a legal no-op. The focused 2/2 behavior trace proves `ContinuousEffectCreated → BlockersDeclared → CardMoved(Exile) → DecisionOpened(SourceLinkedExileCreatureReturn) → CardMoved(Battlefield) → ControllerChanged → AbilityResolved`; both return paths and the invariant audit pass. |

| `circu-colored-cast-exile-restriction-missing` | RAV/core colored-cast trigger and source-bound exile-casting gap — collector `#196` was catalog-only because the engine had neither controller-Blue/controller-Black cast observers nor a source- and card-incarnation-scoped restriction that could reject a later effect-created permission to cast an opponent-owned exiled card. | Fixed; red `237c5c7a`, green `e0ea9aa6` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/cardbench-rav-circu-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-rav --test circu_red -- --nocapture`. Before green it failed `0 passed; 1 failed` with `left: Err(CapabilityGap { collector_number: 196, name: "Circu, Dimir Lobotomist", capability_gap: "card-specific-rules-not-implemented" })` and `right: Ok("RAV-CIRCU-DIMIR-LOBOTOMIST")`. Green adds typed Blue/Black controller-cast conditions, ordinary opponent target decisions, exact top-library exile provenance, and the live source-incarnation restriction. The reviewed trace proves independent Blue and Black exiles, places both triggers for a Blue/Black cast in the normal ordering decision, then rejects a valid effect-created exile permission while Circu is live. Restriction state is removed on source or card zone departure and is invariant-audited. Scope remains narrow: it governs only the represented effect-created exile-cast permission and does not turn unrelated catalog-only printings into executable cards. The verified partition is 279 full / 6 partial / 6 catalog-only unique names (294 / 6 / 6 printings). |
| `return-creature-entry-etb-missing` | Engine reanimation/entry-trigger defect — `ReturnTargetCreatureCardToBattlefieldWithCounterIfManaColorSpent` moved a legal graveyard creature to the battlefield but did not capture any ETB observation. A short-lived returned creature therefore reached the spell's ordinary post-resolution SBA death with no historical ETB event to place. | Fixed; red `822b66d2`, green `50fd952d` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-return-creature-entry-red-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test return_creature_entry_etb_red -- --nocapture`. A zero-cost instant targets a controller-owned 0/0 creature card in their graveyard; the creature has a target-free ETB. Before green the trace was `CardMoved(Battlefield) → SpellResolved → StateBasedAction(zero toughness) → CardMoved(Graveyard)` with no `TriggeredAbilityStacked`, although the public spell resolution returned success. The repair captures the returned object's own entry observation immediately after its new battlefield incarnation exists and before the optional counter or post-resolution SBA. Green retains the historical `source_incarnation: 2` through the 0/0 death, then records `TriggeredAbilityStacked(..., ephemeral-etb)` after the spell's terminal lifecycle/SBA boundary. The focused entry, returned-source-controller, virtual-return, exile-delayed-return, and RAV Vigor Mortis contracts pass. |

| `deterministic-search-entry-etb-missing` | Engine deterministic-library-search / entry-trigger defect — the compatibility deterministic controller-library search moved a selected permanent to the battlefield but registered only a delayed land-entry batch, never the selected permanent's own ETB or controller-scoped entry observers. A short-lived selected creature therefore died to the original spell's post-resolution SBA without its historically observed ETB being placed. | Fixed; red `4503ead7`, green `9ef362f3` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-deterministic-search-entry-red-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test deterministic_search_entry_etb_red -- --nocapture`. A zero-cost instant deterministically finds a 0/0 creature with a target-free ETB and puts it onto the battlefield. Before green the trace records `CardMoved(Battlefield) → LibrarySearchResolved → SpellResolved → StateBasedAction(zero toughness) → CardMoved(Graveyard)`, then no `TriggeredAbilityStacked`; the public rule transition returns success with silently missing trigger provenance. The repair shares the entry-capture pipeline for deterministic Battlefield/BattlefieldTapped results, including land-entry observation. Green records the same entry and SBA death, then `TriggeredAbilityStacked(source incarnation 2, ephemeral-etb)`; deterministic search, policy search, Farseek, and Chord contracts pass. |
| `control-exchange-rechecks-target-power-during-resolution` | Engine stack-resolution defect — `ExchangeControlOfTargetCreatures` correctly validates its controlled/opposing power relation at casting, but rechecked that relation after earlier instructions mutated the same targets. A legal pair could therefore be skipped after resolution had begun. | Fixed; red `5b8259e5`, green `2c70f62b` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-control-exchange-target-red-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test control_exchange_target_snapshot_red -- --nocapture`. A zero-cost instant first gives its controlled 3/3 `-2/+0`, then exchanges that same creature with an opponent's 2/3. All three target occurrences and the `2 <= 3` pair relation are legal at cast and at resolution start. Before green the first modifier resolved, then the trace recorded `TargetInstructionSkipped { effect_index: 1, target: opponent creature }`; controllers remained `(Player 0, Player 1)` because the engine recomputed `2 > 1`. The repair snapshots the pair relation with the initial stack resolution plan and thereafter requires only the original target legality, zone, and incarnation continuity. Green trace is `ContinuousEffectCreated(PowerToughness) → ContinuousEffectCreated(Control) ×2 → ControllerChanged(first) → ControllerChanged(second) → SpellResolved`; the modified first creature remains 1/3, but controllers become `(Player 1, Player 0)`. The focused pair, general target snapshot, later target removal, repeated Exile, and multi-target contracts plus the engine package and strict library Clippy pass. |

| `live-attachment-primitive-bypasses-priority-and-policy` | Engine API-boundary weakness — public `enter_attachment_without_cast` accepted a started game without a priority holder, source action, or policy submission. An external caller could move an Aura out of hand and attach it after another player had priority, with real zone/attachment receipts but no policy provenance. | Fixed; red `43696da6`, green `b8164a41` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-live-attachment-primitive-red-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test live_attachment_primitive_injection_red -- --nocapture`. Player 0 passes priority to Player 1 while controlling a creature and an Aura in hand, then the direct entry primitive used to return `Ok(())`. The repair keeps this bounded fixture helper available before `begin_game` and while the active player has priority, but atomically rejects it after priority has passed. Green evidence is `Err(IllegalAction("a live fixture primitive requires the active player to hold priority"))`; the Aura remains in hand, unattached, and the event log remains only the initial step receipts plus `PriorityPassed`. Rules-owned Aura entry continues through private resolution paths rather than this fixture boundary. |

| `deterministic-search-entry-etb-missing` | Engine deterministic-library-search / entry-trigger defect — the compatibility deterministic controller-library search moved a selected permanent to the battlefield but registered only a delayed land-entry batch, never the selected permanent's own ETB or controller-scoped entry observers. A short-lived selected creature therefore died to the original spell's post-resolution SBA without its historically observed ETB being placed. | Open | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-deterministic-search-entry-red-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test deterministic_search_entry_etb_red -- --nocapture`. A zero-cost instant deterministically finds a 0/0 creature with a target-free ETB and puts it onto the battlefield. Before green the trace records `CardMoved(Battlefield) → LibrarySearchResolved → SpellResolved → StateBasedAction(zero toughness) → CardMoved(Graveyard)`, then no `TriggeredAbilityStacked`; the public rule transition returns success with silently missing trigger provenance. |

| `private-decision-permanent-entry-etb-incarnation` | Engine deferred-permanent-resolution/SBA provenance defect — a permanent spell with a resolving private discard choice remains on the stack until that choice completes. Its terminal continuation moved the permanent to the battlefield, ran SBAs, and only then built its ETB event. A 0/0 entrant was already in the graveyard, so the emitted ETB used its new graveyard incarnation and the invariant audit rolled the entire legal decision back. | Fixed; red `6541adff`, green `58e75106` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/cardbench-private-decision-entry-red-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test permanent_resolution_private_decision_etb_red -- --nocapture`. A zero-cost Flash 0/0 creature targets the opponent with one private discard effect and has a target-free ETB. The opponent chooses their one legal hand card. Before green, submitting that legal decision returns `IllegalAction("ETB trigger source incarnation was not created by battlefield entry")`; atomic rollback prints `private-decision permanent entry result=Err(...)` and retains only the pending private choice. The repair captures permanent and land-entry observations immediately after the deferred entry, then reaches the shared SBA boundary. The green trace retains `chosen_modal_mode=0` and `chosen_color=Red` on one materialized stack effect, records `SpellColorChosen(Red) → SpellModeChosen(0) → SpellCast → PolicyMoveSubmitted(CastWithMode)`, resolves the target to exactly Red, and passes the live and final invariant audits. | `library-search-choice-stale-decision-identity` | Engine generic private-search compatibility defect — the library-search view exposed `DecisionId(2)`, but the legacy response shape omitted it and still completed a later search after the source left and returned. | Fixed; red `a70cdfb1`, green `f3e803e7` | Exact red command: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test library_search_choice_stale_identity_red -- --nocapture`. | `private-opponent-library-choice-stale-decision-identity` | Engine private-decision/state-machine defect — private opponent-library selection was identified only by source/ability/selection, so an old response was accepted after the source bounced and recast. | Fixed; red `86af5ea4`, green `2e9d74f8` | Exact red command: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test private_opponent_library_choice_stale_identity_red -- --nocapture`. | `chorus-of-the-conclave-battlefield-cast-cost-gap` | RAV green coverage gap — Chorus of the Conclave was catalog-only despite requiring a battlefield-only optional any-mana cost for creature spells, paid-amount provenance through resolution, and entry counters only for cast creature spells. | Fixed; red `41141986`, green `e227dbaf` | Exact red command: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/cardbench-rav-chorus-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-rav --test chorus_of_conclave_red -- --nocapture`. | `private-library-choice-stale-decision-identity` | Engine private-decision/state-machine defect — a top-library LOOK choice was identified only by spell object ID and selected cards, so an old response was accepted after the source left and returned with a new incarnation. | Fixed; red `2a0dda74`, green `f4880455` | Exact red command: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test stale_private_library_choice_red -- --nocapture`. | `optional-trigger-stale-choice-identity` | Engine decision/state-machine defect — optional-trigger compatibility views were identified only by source, ability, and mana, so a decline submitted for an older identical trigger was accepted for a newer trigger. | Fixed; red `458dd7bf`, green `40dc00fb` | Exact red command: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test optional_trigger_stale_choice_identity_red -- --nocapture`. | `dimir-cutpurse-combat-trigger-discard-draw-gap` | RAV blue/black coverage gap — Dimir Cutpurse was catalog-only despite the engine needing combat-damage-to-player recipient provenance, a private one-card discard choice, and a controller draw in ordered trigger resolution. | Fixed; red `a5bd26d3`, green `21dcc392` | Exact red command: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/cardbench-rav-dimir-cutpurse-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-rav --test dimir_cutpurse_red -- --nocapture`. | `damage-shield-type-change-target-provenance` | Engine damage-prevention/provenance defect — a shield attached to an Artifact Creature was revalidated against current card type after a legal type-changing effect, so copying an Artifact onto the permanent incorrectly rejected the shielded damage transition. | Fixed; red `581b5ee4`, green `74bf6123` | Exact red command: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test damage_prevention_shield_type_change_red -- --nocapture`. | `virtual-target-library-reorder-terminal-zone-move` | Engine virtual-stack/private-decision lifecycle defect — a copied target-player library reorder opened the correct private decision but its completed continuation attempted to move the virtual copy to a physical terminal zone. | Fixed; red `10665855`, green `59175d5a` | Exact red command: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test virtual_spell_copy_target_library_reorder_red -- --nocapture`. | `transmute-pre-activation-library-visibility` | Engine privacy-boundary defect — ordinary live `GameView` exposed exact transmute library matches before the ability was activated or a private search decision existed. | Fixed; red `3d48f35a`, green `6474bfb8` | Exact red command: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test transmute_pre_activation_visibility_red -- --nocapture`. | `damage-redirection-destination-incarnation-gap` | Engine replacement/provenance defect — a pending damage-redirection destination stored only an object ID. | `svogthos-dynamic-graveyard-animation-missing` | RAV/core continuous-layer coverage gap — Svogthos was catalog-only because source animation could install only fixed base power/toughness, not a controller-captured live creature-card-graveyard count through layer 7b. | Fixed; red `7da086e9`, green `feba1ed0` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/cardbench-rav-svogthos-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-rav --test svogthos_red -- --nocapture`. | `card-view-cannot-attack-restriction-mismatch` | Engine policy-view/state-machine defect — `CardView::can_attack` omitted the current `CannotAttackOrBlock` keyword although `declare_attackers` enforced it. | `phytohydra-damage-replacement-gap` | RAV green coverage gap — Phytohydra was catalog-only even though its damage replacement must prevent damage and place that many +1/+1 counters, while explicitly unpreventable damage must bypass the replacement. | Fixed; red `32aaf445`, green `82e2dd92` | Exact red command: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/cardbench-rav-phytohydra-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-rav --test phytohydra_red -- --nocapture`. | `must-block-landwalk-false-legal-blocker` | Engine combat-restriction defect — must-block validation ignored the attacker's current landwalk set while scanning for an otherwise legal unassigned blocker. The fix shares landwalk’s defender-land check with must-block availability; green records the legal empty `BlockersDeclared`. |

| `must-block-derived-controller-omitted` | Engine combat/control-layer defect — must-block validation scanned the defending owner's battlefield vector rather than every creature currently controlled by the defending player. It therefore accepted an empty block declaration even when the defender controlled a legal opponent-owned creature. | Fixed; red `e3e325d1`, green `272a060a` | Exact red command: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test must_block_landwalk_red -- --nocapture`. | `departing-eot-control-effect-exiles-opponent-permanent` | Engine multiplayer/control-effect lifecycle defect — a current-turn control effect from a resolved spell remained active while CR 800.4a scanned permanents controlled by the player who left. Its direct trace is `SpellManaPaid → SpellCast → PriorityPassed ×2 → CardMoved(Graveyard) ×2 → LifeGained(2) → SpellResolved → CardMoved(source Graveyard)`; a short-library X=3 probe mills one card but still records `LifeGained(3)`, and final invariants pass. |

| `virtual-spell-copy-counter-unless-target-provenance` | Engine counter/stack provenance defect — ordinary counter spells can target a virtual spell copy, but counter-unless suspension reads the lower stack spell as a physical `CardObject` to capture its incarnation. | `virtual-spell-copy-counter-unless-decision-continuation` | Engine counter/decision-continuation provenance defect — after counter-unless correctly opened its public payment decision for a virtual target, submitting a legal decline re-read that target as a physical card while validating the continuation. | Fixed; red `7715e6e9`, green `3153849d` | Exact red command: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test virtual_spell_copy_counter_unless_red -- --nocapture`. | `virtual-spell-copy-damage-replacement-choice-bypassed` | Engine stack/replacement-decision provenance defect — a virtual copied targeted damage spell had frozen source colors on its live stack object, but the replacement suspension path re-read its stack-only source as a physical card. | `virtual-spell-copy-library-search-terminal-zone-move` | Engine stack/private-decision lifecycle defect — a virtual copied policy-submitted library search correctly exposes only its controller's legal hidden candidates, but its completed continuation records `SpellResolved` and moves the virtual source to a physical zone. | `virtual-copy-spell-retarget-terminal-zone-move` | Engine stack/copy-decision lifecycle defect — a virtual copy of a copying spell opens its own legal `SpellCopyTargets` decision and creates its child copy, but its completed continuation records `SpellResolved` and tries to move the virtual source to a physical zone. Submitting the legal new player target returned `Err(UnknownCard(ObjectId(4)))` after the decision-open receipt, because the copying spell's terminal continuation attempted a fabricated physical spell-zone move. The repair creates the selected child virtual copy then gives its virtual parent its own terminal receipt. Green trace is `DecisionCompleted → SpellCopied(child) → SpellCopyResolved(parent)`; final invariants pass. |

| `virtual-spell-copy-graveyard-creature-return-terminal-zone-move` | Engine stack/public-decision lifecycle defect — a virtual copied creature-return spell correctly runs one public choice for each affected player, but after the final legal selection its continuation records `SpellResolved` and moves the stack-only copy to a physical zone. | `virtual-spell-copy-graveyard-land-return-terminal-zone-move` | Engine stack/public-decision lifecycle defect — a virtual copied spell correctly opens and validates its public graveyard-land selection, but the completed continuation records `SpellResolved` and tries to move the stack-only copy to a physical card zone. | `agrus-kos-attacking-color-modifiers-missing` | RAV/core combat-effect gap — Agrus Kos was catalog-only because the engine could not bind an attack trigger to a declared-attacker subset filtered by each recipient's current color. Reusing a controller-wide anthem would incorrectly modify nonattackers. | Fixed; red `ea4fb31a`, green `864253d2` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/cardbench-magic-target CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 cargo test -p cardbench-magic-rav --test agrus_kos_red -- --nocapture`. It failed 0/1 at `Agrus Kos definition exists`. The green adds the expansion-neutral target-free `ModifyAttackingCreaturesOfColorUntilEndOfTurn` effect and invariant: it is legal only in a target-free `Attacks` binding with nonzero ordinary-color modifiers. Resolution snapshots only current declared attackers, filters live characteristics per independent color, and installs ordinary end-of-turn layer-seven effects. The reviewed trace is `AttackersDeclared → TriggeredAbilityStacked → ContinuousEffectCreated ×6 → AbilityResolved`; the full direct trace later records `ContinuousEffectExpired ×6` at cleanup. Agrus and another red/white attacker receive both modifiers, a red attacker receives only the red modifier, a green/white attacker receives only the white modifier, and the same-color nonattacker remains unchanged. Public scenario `rav_agrus_kos_attack_color_modifiers` has digest `fnv1a64:29b052fe6ec09e66`; final invariant audits pass. |

| `virtual-spell-copy-conditional-discard-terminal-zone-move` | Engine stack/private-decision lifecycle defect — a copied draw-then-discard spell correctly draws cards and opens the target player's private `ConditionalPrivateDiscard` choice, but its terminal continuation records `SpellResolved` and tries to move the stack-only source to a card zone. | `virtual-spell-copy-library-partition-terminal-zone-move` | Engine stack/private-decision lifecycle defect — a virtual copied private top-library partition spell opens and validates the exact controller-only decision, but terminal resolution still records `SpellResolved` and moves its stack-only identity to a card zone. | `virtual-spell-copy-library-reorder-terminal-zone-move` | Engine stack/public-decision lifecycle defect — a virtual copied reorder spell correctly opens and validates its public `LibraryReorder` decision, but its completed terminal continuation records `SpellResolved` and tries to move the stack-only copy to a card zone. | `virtual-spell-copy-private-library-choice-source-rejected` | Engine stack/private-decision provenance defect — a copied spell that resolves through the private library-choice state machine reads its stack-only copy as a physical card to emit `CardsLookedAt`, so it cannot open its mandatory no-priority decision. | `virtual-spell-copy-combat-prevention-source-rejected` | Engine combat-replacement/provenance defect — virtual instant copies cannot create either represented independent combat-prevention record because both constructors require a physical source object while the copy resolves. | Fixed; red `3082bb4a`, green `1671de46` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test virtual_spell_copy_combat_prevention_red -- --nocapture`. Green traces are `SpellCopied(copy=4) → CombatDamagePreventionCreated(source=4,creature=1) → SpellCopyResolved` and `SpellCopied(copy=3) → GlobalCombatDamagePreventionCreated(source=3) → SpellCopyResolved`; both final invariant audits pass. |

| `virtual-spell-copy-targeted-damage-shield-source-rejected` | Engine replacement/provenance defect — a copied instant may legally create a source-independent target-side prevention shield, but `install_damage_prevention_shield` requires a physical source object after the virtual copy has begun resolving. | `virtual-spell-copy-temporary-continuous-effect-source-rejected` | Engine stack/layer provenance defect — a copied instant or sorcery can legally create a fixed-duration continuous effect, but `install_continuous_effect` requires a physical source object after the resolver has popped the virtual copy. Resolution rolls back rather than retaining its immutable source provenance until end of turn. | Fixed; red `2ce65711`, green `d5448837` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test virtual_spell_copy_continuous_effect_red -- --nocapture`. Source-relative control, attachment redirection, and activated-ability grants remain rejected. Green trace is `SpellCopied(copy=4) → ContinuousEffectCreated(source=4,target=1,layer=Control) → ControllerChanged(0→1) → SpellCopyResolved`; the copied effect controls the creature for the turn and the final invariant audit passes. |

| `privileged-position-other-permanent-shroud-missing` | RAV/core static-effect and target-legality gap — Privileged Position is catalog-only because the engine has no permanent-wide Shroud keyword or source-excluding controller-static effect. Both focused contracts fail before gameplay: `Privileged Position definition exists` and `UnknownDefinition("RAV-PRIVILEGED-POSITION")`. Green adds source-excluding layer-six Shroud for every other controlled battlefield permanent and routes all permanent target checks through the same selection and resolution-recheck predicate. Reviewed trace has no `SpellCast` for the rejected targeting spell, then `ManaAbilityActivated(Black) → ManaAdded → ManaAbilityActivated(White) → ManaAdded → SpellCast(Leave No Trace) → CardDestroyed(Privileged Position) → SpellResolved`; the source stays targetable and the final invariant audit passes. Public scenario `rav_privileged_position_other_permanent_shroud`, digest `fnv1a64:916d6eb428b6564e`, is in the 189-scenario parity corpus. |


| `virtual-spell-copy-damage-controller-provenance-lost` | Engine damage/controller-provenance defect — source-controller prevention treats a virtual copied spell as if it had no source controller. The repair persists the copied stack controller in private virtual-copy provenance and audits it against the live stack object. Green trace ends `DamagePrevented { source: ObjectId(4), target: Permanent(ObjectId(1)), amount: 2 } → SpellCopyResolved`; the ward has zero damage and the lower physical spell remains independent. |

| `virtual-spell-copy-automatic-damage-replacement-source-lost` | Engine damage/replacement provenance defect — direct stack damage passes frozen source colors, but the automatic amount-replacement path re-reads a virtual copy as a physical card. Any live automatic replacement therefore aborts resolution with `UnknownCard(copy)` rather than applying to the copied packet. | Fixed; red `74436cf0`, green `c886790d` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test virtual_spell_copy_damage_replacement_red -- --nocapture`. The first copy-effect resolves, but the two passes for the virtual copy returned `Err(UnknownCard(ObjectId(4)))`; the atomic transition rolled back. Green trace records `DamageReplacementApplied(HalveDamage) → DamageAmountReplaced { source: copy, original_amount: 3, replacement_amount: 1 } → DamageDealtToPlayer { source: copy, amount: 1 } → SpellCopyResolved`; final invariant audit passes. |

| `virtual-spell-copy-chain-retarget-skipped` | Engine copy/decision-state-machine defect — a virtual instant or sorcery is now a legal copy target, but the suspended “may choose new targets” path still excluded virtual originals. The copying effect consequently skipped its public `DecisionId` boundary and silently retained old targets. | Fixed; red `f24a3760`, green `4aa36cfe` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test virtual_spell_copy_chain_retarget_red -- --nocapture`. The repair routes a live virtual original through the same suspended public decision state machine as a physical original. Green event evidence: `DecisionOpened(SpellCopyTargets) → DecisionCompleted → SpellCopied(copy=5, original=4, retargeted=true) → SpellCopyResolved(5,4)`; the child damages the chosen controller, while the retained first copy and physical original each damage their original target. Final invariant audit passes. |

| `virtual-spell-copy-chain-rejected` | Engine stack/copy substrate gap — a virtual spell copy is a spell, but the copy constructor rejected it as an original and the invariant assumed every virtual copy directly referenced a physical spell. Player 1 legally casts a second copy effect targeting that virtual spell, but the two priority passes that resolve it return `IllegalAction("only a physical instant or sorcery spell can be copied in this slice")`. The repair permits an instant/sorcery virtual copy as the immediate original, freezes that copy's existing target, X, modal, color, source-incarnation, and catalog-definition provenance onto the child, and audits an acyclic definition-consistent ancestry chain. Event replay additionally requires a virtual immediate original to have an earlier nonterminal `SpellCopied` receipt. Green trace: `SpellCopied(copy=4, original=1) → SpellCopied(copy=5, original=4) → DamageDealtToPlayer(source=5, amount=1) → SpellCopyResolved(5,4) → DamageDealtToPlayer(source=4, amount=1) → SpellCopyResolved(4,1) → DamageDealtToPlayer(source=1, amount=1) → SpellResolved(1)`; the target finishes at 17 and the final invariant audit passes. |

| `virtual-spell-copy-inherits-spent-mana` | Engine copy/provenance defect — the virtual-copy constructor cloned the original spell’s explicit `mana_spent`, even though a spell copy is not cast and mana is not a copied value. Any represented “if mana of this color was spent” instruction could execute for a copy solely because its original was paid with that color. | Fixed; red `bc240178`, green `70498e16` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test virtual_spell_copy_mana_spent_red -- --nocapture`. Player 0 pays blue mana for a generic one-mana instant with `DrawControllerIfManaColorSpent { Blue }`; Player 1 copies it. The virtual copy resolves first and incorrectly moves Player 1's library card to hand. Red trace: `SpellManaPaid { player: PlayerId(0), card: ObjectId(1), colors: [Blue] } → SpellCopied { copy: ObjectId(6), original: ObjectId(1), controller: PlayerId(1) } → CardMoved { card: ObjectId(4), to: Hand } → SpellCopyResolved`. The repair preserves copied choices (mode, targets, X, chosen color) but clears payment-only `mana_spent`, Convoke-symbol, and cost-reduction fields; X-copy invariant validation no longer demands an invented mana receipt. Reviewed green trace has no `CardMoved` for Player 1’s library card before `SpellCopyResolved`, leaving it in `Library`. |

| `survivor-virtual-copy-loses-departed-original` | Engine multiplayer/virtual-stack invariant defect — CR 800.4a removes the departing owner’s physical spell, but a virtual spell copy controlled by a surviving player is a distinct stack object and must remain capable of resolving. The invariant instead required every virtual copy to retain a lower live physical original, rolling back the player-loss transition once that original left the game. | Fixed; red `b9883023`, green `60024a6d` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test survivor_virtual_copy_owner_departure_red -- --nocapture`. Player 0 casts a one-damage instant; player 1 copies it, then resolves a 20-damage spell eliminating player 0 above the survivor-controlled copy. The loss-resolution pass returns `Err(IllegalAction("virtual stack spell lacks a lower physical original"))`. The repair freezes the copied catalog definition in the private virtual-copy provenance at copy creation, resolves views and effect validation from that immutable definition, and rejects self/copy-chain or definition-mismatched metadata. Reviewed green trace: `PlayerLost(0) → ObjectLeftGame(original) → PriorityPassed(1) → PriorityPassed(2) → DamageDealtToPlayer(copy,target,1) → SpellCopyResolved`; player 2 is at 19 and the final invariant audit passes. |

| `departing-owner-removes-survivor-controlled-ability` | Engine multiplayer/stack lifecycle defect — CR 800.4a cleanup removed every stack item whose physical source was owned by the departing player, even an activated ability controlled by a surviving player. The subsequent activation-lifecycle invariant correctly detected an unclosed `AbilityActivated` receipt and rolled back the loss resolution. | Fixed; red `88315f3f`, green `3ba6d56a` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test departing_owner_controlled_ability_red -- --nocapture`. In a three-player game, player 1 steals player 0's pinger and activates its ability targeting player 2, then resolves a 20-damage spell that eliminates player 0 above it. Red fails the lethal-resolution pass with `IllegalAction("ability activation and terminal receipts disagree with the live stack")`; the rollback log ends at the two surviving priority passes above the lethal spell. Green retains only an `ability_id` stack item controlled by a survivor, freezes exact characteristics/controller provenance before deleting its source, permits that narrow missing-source stack state, and removes it at the first later SBA fixed point after the ability terminal receipt. Reviewed trace: `PlayerLost(0) → ObjectLeftGame(pinger) → PriorityPassed(1) → PriorityPassed(2) → DamageDealtToPlayer(target,1) → AbilityResolved`; target life is 19, stack is empty, and the next survivor's turn begins. |

| `returned-damage-source-loses-controller-provenance` | Engine source-controller/LKI defect — an old activated ability correctly retained its source object id and former incarnation, but controller-relative damage prevention re-read the controller of a later battlefield incarnation with that id. A returned-and-stolen source could turn its already pending friendly damage into opposing damage. | Fixed; red `50758505`, green `836ed999` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test returned_source_controller_lki_red -- --nocapture`. Player 0 activates a one-damage ability targeting a creature protected from sources its controller controls. Player 1 destroys the source, player 0 returns it, then player 1 steals the new incarnation before the old ability resolves. Red records `DamageDealtToPermanent { amount: 1 }` instead of `DamagePrevented`, and fails `left: 1, right: 0`. Per CR 113.7a/113.8 and 608.2h, the old ability cannot read a returned object as its original source. Green freezes an exact controller beside every characteristic LKI record, requires a one-to-one valid-object/incarnation pairing, and reads that controller only if the source no longer has the resolving incarnation. The reviewed trace has old incarnation `1`, returned incarnation `3`, a later `ControllerChanged(0→1)` for the new object, then `DamagePrevented { amount: 1 } → AbilityResolved` for the old ability; unchanged live source incarnations still use their current controller. |

| `last-known-characteristics-orphan-on-player-departure` | Engine object-lifecycle/LKI defect — CR 800.4a player departure removes owned objects directly, but the new private last-known-characteristics provenance map retained entries for those deleted object ids. Its deliberate object-existence invariant then rejected and rolled back an otherwise terminal loss transition. | Fixed; red `7803c434`, green `5be37fbe` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test last_known_departure_red -- --nocapture`. A player casts a 20-damage instant targeting themself; casting snapshots the hand incarnation, resolution causes `PlayerLost`, and owner-departure removes the spell object. Red fails `IllegalAction("last-known characteristic provenance has an invalid object incarnation")` at the second priority pass, with only the pre-resolution `SpellCast`, incarnation-advance, and first `PriorityPassed` receipts surviving rollback. Green retires every LKI record for an object exactly when owner departure deletes its object record, leaving the strict LKI existence invariant intact and preserving provenance for non-owned cards that player departure exiles instead. The focused green trace is `DamageDealtToPlayer(20) → SpellResolved → CardMoved(Graveyard) → PlayerLost → ObjectLeftGame → GameEnded`; the terminal game and invariant audit both pass. |

| `damage-replacement-candidates-lose-source-incarnation` | Engine replacement/LKI defect — direct damage commitment retained the old source incarnation, but concurrent replacement candidate discovery still read the returned source's current characteristics. A source whose old ability had `DamageCannotBePrevented` incorrectly exposed an affected-player prevention choice after it returned without that quality. | Fixed; red `32413ccc`, green `31ee6392` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test deathtouch_source_lki_red returned_source_cannot_offer_prevention_against_old_unpreventable_damage -- --nocapture`. A temporary-unpreventable two-damage source is destroyed and returned before resolution while the target has a two-point shield and a non-prevention halve replacement. Red opens a decision with `[HalveDamage, TargetedShield]`; only halving should apply automatically. Green carries the exact source incarnation through candidate discovery at stack suspension, global packet scan/resumption, public choice creation, submission revalidation, and application. The reviewed trace has `pending=None`, then `DamageReplacementApplied(HalveDamage) → DamageAmountReplaced(2→1) → DamageDealtToPermanent(1) → AbilityResolved`; the shield is never a candidate. Focused source-LKI/global-source-color tests, strict Clippy, fmt, and diff checks pass. |

| `activated-damage-source-lki-wrong-incarnation-after-return` | Engine source-characteristic/incarnation defect — departed damage-source lookup correctly froze a former incarnation, but selected the live characteristics whenever the same `ObjectId` later re-entered the battlefield. An old activated ability could therefore lose source quality when its source was returned before resolution. | Fixed; red `94ad586d`, green `b75d791c` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test deathtouch_source_lki_red returned_source_cannot_replace_its_old_deathtouch_damage_identity -- --nocapture`. A temporary-Deathtouch creature activates a one-damage ability, is destroyed, then returns to the battlefield as a new non-Deathtouch incarnation before the old ability resolves. The red target incorrectly remains on the battlefield. Green carries the source incarnation through direct and global damage commitment, direct prevention, automatic amount replacements, and source-quality checks. A live source may supply characteristics only when its exact incarnation agrees with the stack source; otherwise the old-incarnation LKI record is mandatory. The reviewed trace has old incarnation `1`, returned incarnation `3`, then `DamageDealtToPermanent → AbilityResolved → StateBasedAction(reason=deathtouch) → CardMoved(Graveyard)` for the target. Focused LKI, damage-cannot-be-prevented, and combat Deathtouch tests plus strict Clippy, fmt, and diff checks pass. |

| `activated-damage-deathtouch-source-lki-missing` | Engine source-characteristic/LKI defect — an activated ability that causes its source to deal damage re-read the departed source's current graveyard characteristics at resolution. A temporary Deathtouch ability that applied to the source immediately before it left therefore vanished, even though the damage source must use that battlefield incarnation's last-known information. | Fixed; red `ba0eb296`, green `8f147500` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test deathtouch_source_lki_red -- --nocapture`. A source gains Deathtouch through a permanent continuous effect, activates a one-damage creature ability, and is destroyed in response. The red source is correctly in the graveyard and no longer currently has Deathtouch, but the target remains on the battlefield after the resolving one-damage ability. Green freezes private `Characteristics` under the exact former `(ObjectId, incarnation)` before every zone transition, validates that provenance cannot name a current/future incarnation or a colorless card-color set, and uses it for departed damage-source qualities. The reviewed trace is `DamageDealtToPermanent(1) → AbilityResolved → StateBasedAction(reason=deathtouch) → CardMoved(Graveyard)` for the target. Focused Deathtouch combat, damage-cannot-be-prevented, global source-color, strict Clippy, fmt, and diff checks pass. |

| `global-damage-replacement-loses-stack-source-colors` | Engine stack/replacement provenance defect — stack objects retained a source's activation-time colors, but a global damage replacement decision recomputed source-color prevention from the live source. | `redirected-combat-creature-trigger-provenance-missing` | Engine combat trigger-lifecycle defect — direct combat damage observes only its originally assigned creature. | `combat-replacement-resolver-strict-lint-regression` | Engine maintainability/invariant-audit regression — the broadened combat replacement resolver exceeds the repository's strict Clippy function-size boundary, so the engine cannot pass its required static audit. | Fixed; red `5f3d4172`, green `36385d9f` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `cargo clippy -p cardbench-magic-engine -p cardbench-magic-rav --lib -- -D warnings`. | `simultaneous-sacrifice-cost-lki-is-incomplete` | Engine activated-cost / trigger-lifecycle defect — one activation cost that sacrifices multiple creatures transitions them sequentially. | `simultaneous-opponent-graveyard-lki-is-incomplete` | Engine simultaneous zone-change/trigger-lifecycle defect — the new destruction batch froze creature-departure observers but left `OpponentCardPutIntoGraveyard` on the individual post-move path. | `simultaneous-destroy-all-lki-is-incomplete` | Engine simultaneous zone-change/trigger-lifecycle defect — a single destroy-all instruction snapshots its recipients but invokes the normal destruction transition one permanent at a time. Generic creature-departure observers are therefore sampled after earlier recipients have already left, so a dying observer misses a different creature's simultaneous death. | Fixed; red `a814c739`, green `2efbfe39` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test simultaneous_destroy_all_lki_red -- --nocapture`. | `blocker-evasion-snapshots-attacker-too-early` | Engine combat timing defect — the attacker declaration captured Flying, Fear, black-only evasion, unblockable, landwalk, and must-block markers, and blocker declaration reused those stale sets after players received priority. The red action returns `Ok(())` and writes `BlockersDeclared { assignments: [(ObjectId(1), ObjectId(2))] }`; correct rules reject it because evasion is checked at blocker declaration. The reviewed trace now returns `Err(IllegalAction("flying attacker can be blocked only by flying or reach"))` and emits no `BlockersDeclared`; existing flying/reach and mutual-lethal provenance contracts remain green. |

| `simultaneous-another-dies-lki-is-incomplete` | Engine SBA/trigger-lifecycle defect — lethal creatures are removed one at a time within a single state-based-action pass. `AnotherCreatureDies` samples only currently live battlefield observers for each individual move, so the first creature is already absent when the second dies and cannot observe that simultaneous death through last-known information. | Fixed; red `90efd114`, green `3cf026a0` | Exact red command from `varieties/magic`: `cargo test -p cardbench-magic-engine --test simultaneous_another_dies_lki_red -- --nocapture`. The reviewed trace has both historical sources (`ObjectId(1)` and `ObjectId(2)`) and preserves their exact battlefield incarnation; all existing generic leave/dies, fixed-point, and invariant contracts remain green. |

| `removed-attacker-retains-live-blocker-provenance` | Engine combat/invariant defect — removing a blocked attacker correctly dropped its blocker map, but retained those former blockers in Flying/Fear/black-evasion qualification sets and in first-strike-source provenance. | `vigilance-declaration-snapshot-rejects-legal-keyword-change` | Engine combat/invariant defect — a creature that had Vigilance when attackers were declared may legally lose that keyword before combat ends; it stays untapped, because declaration is not retroactive. | `private-library-look-event-log-leak` | Engine policy-visibility / hidden-zone defect — resolving `LookAtTopCardsChooseForLifeOrGraveyard` correctly withheld the pending candidates from an opponent `GameView`, but its public `canonical_event_log()` serialized the exact hidden `ObjectId`s in `CardsLookedAt`. The reviewed green trace is `CardsLookedAt { viewer: PlayerId(0), source: ObjectId(1), source_incarnation: 2, count: 1 }`; the opponent view remains empty, the hidden `ObjectId(2)` is absent from the canonical transcript, and the private-choice invariant passes. | `congregation-at-dawn-ordered-library-search-missing` | RAV card-fidelity / hidden-zone ordering gap — the generic multi-card library search could move selected cards to hand or battlefield, but it could not preserve a policy-submitted selected order through the required shuffle and then place those same revealed cards on the library top without inventing zone transitions.
